131	  check('白天过岩石层整链消散', enemies.every((e) => e.dead));

133	console.log(`\n结果: ${ok} 通过, ${bad} 失败`);

343	    } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
344	      e.ai2 += 1;
345	      if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 8 : 25)) {
346	        e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
347	        if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
348	        if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
349	      } else {
350	        e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
351	      }
352	      if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 42 : 70)) {
353	        e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
354	        e.spin = target;
355	        if (e.ai3 >= (spaz ? 10 : 4)) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
356	        else e.ai1 = 1;
357	      }
358	    }
359	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) { e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
360	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
361	    return;
362	  }
363	
364	  // ---- 变身(ai0=1 自旋加速 / ai0=2 减速,各 100t;ai2=角速度 0↔0.5) ----
365	  if (e.ai0 === 1 || e.ai0 === 2) {
366	    if (e.ai0 === 1) { e.ai2 += 0.005; if (e.ai2 > 0.5) e.ai2 = 0.5; }
367	    else { e.ai2 -= 0.005; if (e.ai2 < 0) e.ai2 = 0; }
368	    e.spin += e.ai2;
369	    e.ai1 += 1;
370	    if (e.ai1 >= 100) {
371	      e.ai0 += 1; e.ai1 = 0;
372	      if (e.ai0 === 3) e.ai2 = 0;
373	      else {
374	        game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);
375	        game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#FF6040', 20, 2);
376	      }
377	    }
378	    e.vx *= 0.98; e.vy *= 0.98;
379	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
380	    if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
381	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
382	    return;
383	  }
384	
385	  // ---- 三阶段(ai0=3):伤害×1.5,防御 +(雷眼10/魔眼18) ----
386	  const b = baseOf(e);
387	  e.def.damage = Math.round(b.dmg * 1.5);
388	  e.def.defense = b.def + (spaz ? 18 : 10);
389	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
390	    // 上方悬浮:雷眼 玩家正上 300(8/0.15,300t→冲);魔眼 侧 180 同高(4/0.1,400t→冲)+ 火焰喷射 101
391	    const spd = spaz ? 4 : 8, acc = spaz ? 0.1 : 0.15;
392	    const offX = spaz ? 180 : 0, offY = spaz ? 0 : 300;
393	    const side = e.cx < player.x + player.w ? -1 : 1;
394	    seek(e, player.cx + side * offX, player.cy - offY, spd, acc);
395	    e.ai2 += 1;
396	    if (e.ai2 >= (spaz ? 400 : 300)) { e.ai1 = 1; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
397	    e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
398	    if (e.aiT % (spaz ? 8 : 180) === 0 && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
399	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
400	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
401	      const spdP = spaz ? 6 : 8.5;
402	      const dmgP = spaz ? 30 : 25;
403	      const pid = spaz ? 101 : 100;
404	      const lead = spaz ? 4 : 15;
405	      shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * lead, e.cy + dy / d * lead,
406	        dx / d * spdP + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dy / d * spdP + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dmgP, pid);
407	    }
408	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
409	    // 冲撞:雷眼 侧 340 8/0.2 / 180t 循环;魔眼 直冲 14 速(50/80t 减速,连 6)
410	    if (spaz) {
411	      e.spin = target;
412	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
413	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
414	      e.vx = dx / d * 14; e.vy = dy / d * 14;
415	      e.ai1 = 2;
416	    } else {
417	      const side = e.cx < player.x + player.w ? -1 : 1;
418	      seek(e, player.cx + side * 340, player.cy, 8, 0.2);
419	      e.spin = Math.atan2(player.cy - e.cy, player.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
420	      if (e.aiT % 60 === 0 && losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
421	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
422	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
423	        shoot(game, e.cx + dx / d * 15, e.cy + dy / d * 15, dx / d * 9, dy / d * 9, 18, 100);
424	      }
425	      e.ai2 += 1;
426	      if (e.ai2 >= 180) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
427	    }
428	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2 && spaz) {
429	    // 魔眼冲撞减速段(50t 起阻尼,80t 一循环,6 循环回悬浮)
430	    e.ai2 += 1;
431	    if (e.ai2 >= 50) {
432	      e.vx *= 0.93; e.vy *= 0.93;
433	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
434	      if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
435	    } else {
436	      e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
437	    }
438	    if (e.ai2 >= 80) {
439	      e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
440	      e.spin = target;
441	      if (e.ai3 >= 6) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; } else e.ai1 = 1;
442	    }
443	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
444	    // 雷眼冲撞减速段(25t 起阻尼,70t 一循环,4 循环回悬浮)
445	    e.ai2 += 1;
446	    if (e.ai2 >= 25) {
447	      e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
448	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
449	      if (Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) e.vy = 0;
450	    } else {
451	      e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) - Math.PI / 2;
452	    }
453	    if (e.ai2 >= 70) {
454	      e.ai3 += 1; e.ai2 = 0;
455	      e.spin = target;
456	      if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; } else e.ai1 = 1;
457	    }
458	  }
459	  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
460	}
461	
462	/** AI_032 机械骷髅王头 127(NPC.cs:27736-28054):初生成 4 部件;
463	 *  ai1: 0=悬浮(Y 带 200-500/X 窗 ±100,600t)→1=旋冲(防伤×2,2 速,400t);
464	 *  2=白天狂暴(伤/防 9999,10+距离/100 速追杀);3=玩家死/远 6000px 离场。 */
465	export function skeletronPrimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
466	  // 初始化:生成 128/129/130/131(ai0=侧 ±1, ai1=头 id;130/131 ai3=150 预热)
467	  if (!e.bInit) {
468	    e.bInit = true;
469	    const specs: Array<[number, number]> = [[128, -1], [129, 1], [130, -1], [131, 1]];
470	    for (const [id, side] of specs) {
471	      const p = spawnPart(game, e, id);
472	      if (p) { p.ai0 = side; p.ai1 = e.id; p.ai3 = id === 130 || id === 131 ? 150 : 0; p.bInit = true; }
473	    }
474	  }
475	  // 每 tick 从基值重置(vanilla damage=defDamage/defense=defDefense 置顶)
476	  const b = baseOf(e);
477	  e.def.damage = b.dmg;
478	  e.def.defense = b.def;
479	  // 玩家死亡(=无有效目标):按离场处理(原版 TargetClosest 落空 → ai[1]=3)
480	  if (!player) e.ai1 = 3;
481	  // 玩家死/超 6000px → 离场
482	  if (!player || Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) > 6000 || Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 6000) e.ai1 = 3;
483	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
484	  // 白天 → 狂暴(原版不逃走,持续 9999 追杀;Game.ts 日出剔除对 127 豁免)
485	  if (game.world.clock.isDay && e.ai1 !== 3 && e.ai1 !== 2) {
486	    e.ai1 = 2;
487	    game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
488	  }
489	  if (e.ai1 === 0) {
490	    // 悬浮段(:27787-27874):Y 带 [玩家-200, 玩家-500] 0.1/±2;X 窗 ±100 0.1/±8
491	    e.ai2 += 1;
492	    if (e.ai2 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; }
493	    e.spin = e.vx / 15;
494	    if (e.y > player.y - 200) {
495	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
496	      e.vy -= 0.1;
497	      if (e.vy > 2) e.vy = 2;
498	    } else if (e.y < player.y - 500) {
499	      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.98;
500	      e.vy += 0.1;
501	      if (e.vy < -2) e.vy = -2;
502	    }
503	    if (e.cx > player.cx + 100) {
504	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
505	      e.vx -= 0.1;
506	      if (e.vx > 8) e.vx = 8;
507	    }
508	    if (e.cx < player.cx - 100) {
509	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;
510	      e.vx += 0.1;
511	      if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8;
512	    }
513	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
514	    // 旋冲段(:27875-27970):防伤×2,spin+=0.3*dir,经典 2 速直冲,400t 回悬浮
515	    e.def.defense = b.def * 2;
516	    e.def.damage = b.dmg * 2;
517	    e.ai2 += 1;
518	    if (e.ai2 === 2) game.playSfx('roar', 0.6);
519	    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
520	    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
521	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
522	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
523	    e.vx = dx / d * 2; e.vy = dy / d * 2;
524	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
525	    // 白天狂暴(:27971-28004):伤/防 9999,速 10+dist/100 钳 [8,32]
526	    e.def.damage = 9999;
527	    e.def.defense = 9999;
528	    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
529	    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
530	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
531	    let spd = 10 + d / 100;
532	    spd = Math.max(8, Math.min(32, spd));
533	    e.vx = dx / d * spd; e.vy = dy / d * spd;
534	  } else {
535	    // 离场(:28043-28052):下坠离场
536	    e.vy += 0.1;
537	    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;
538	    e.vx *= 0.95;
539	    if (e.vy > 13) e.vy = 13;
540	  }
541	  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
542	  e.x += e.vx;
543	  e.y += e.vy;
544	}
545	
546	/** AI_033-036 Prime 四部件(NPC.cs:28055-29110):ai1=头 id,ai0=侧 ±1。
547	 *  33=锯 129(上冲俯冲 22 速)/34=激光 130(直冲 20 速,ai3 初始 150 预热)/
548	 *  35=钳 128(悬头侧,激光 102 0 伤)/36=炮 131(玩家上空悬停,激光 100 25 伤)。
549	 *  公共:头失效→自毁;头离场(ai1==3)→自毁;远离锚点 800px→追赶态 99。 */
550	export function primePartAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
551	  const headId = e.ai1;
552	  const head = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === headId && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 32);
553	  // 头死亡/失效 → 自毁(原版 ai2+=10 到 50 清除;简化即时)
554	  if (!head || head.dead) { e.dead = true; return; }
555	  const side = e.ai0 || 1;
556	  e.facing = -side;
557	  const ax = head.cx - 200 * side, ay = head.y + 230; // 部件锚点(vanilla num528 系)
558	  const distA = Math.hypot(ax - e.cx, ay - e.cy);
559	  if (e.ai2 !== 99) { if (distA > 800) e.ai2 = 99; }
560	  else if (distA < 400) e.ai2 = 0;
561	  if (head.ai1 === 3) { e.dead = true; return; } // 头离场:部件跟随消散
562	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; } // 玩家死亡:悬停不动(原版坠落的简化)
563	
564	  const style = e.vanilla?.aiStyle ?? 33;
565	  const hoverBand = (yLow: number, yHigh: number, acc: number, max: number) => {
566	    // Y 带 [head.y+yLow, head.y+yHigh] 内归位(vanilla 双侧 if)
567	    if (e.y > head.y + yLow) {
568	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
569	      e.vy -= acc;
570	      if (e.vy > max) e.vy = max;
571	    } else if (e.y < head.y + yHigh) {
572	      if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96;
573	      e.vy += acc;
574	      if (e.vy < -max) e.vy = -max;
575	    }
576	  };
577	  const xWindow = (winHigh: number, winLow: number, acc: number, max: number) => {
578	    // X 窗 [head.cx+winLow, head.cx+winHigh] 内归位(头侧偏移由调用方传正值)
579	    if (e.cx > head.cx + winHigh) {
580	      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
581	      e.vx -= acc;
582	      if (e.vx > max) e.vx = max;
583	    } else if (e.cx < head.cx + winLow) {
584	      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96;
585	      e.vx += acc;
586	      if (e.vx < -max) e.vx = -max;
587	    }
588	  };
589	
590	  if (e.ai2 === 99) {
591	    // 追赶态(:28083-28132):直奔头(0.5/±12 X,0.1/±8 Y)
592	    if (e.y > head.y) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.96; e.vy -= 0.1; if (e.vy > 8) e.vy = 8; }
593	    else if (e.y < head.y) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.96; e.vy += 0.1; if (e.vy < -8) e.vy = -8; }
594	    if (e.cx > head.cx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx -= 0.5; if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12; }
595	    if (e.cx < head.cx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx += 0.5; if (e.vx < -12) e.vx = -12; }
596	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
597	    return;
598	  }
599	
600	  if (style === 33) {
601	    // ---- 锯(NPC 129,:28134-28358) ----
602	    if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
603	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
604	        // 头冲撞中:7 速逼近玩家(0.05 步进/0.97 阻尼)
605	        seekSteer(e, player, 7, 0.05);
606	        e.ai3 += 1;
607	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
608	      } else {
609	        e.ai3 += 1;
610	        if (e.ai3 >= 300) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
611	        hoverBand(320, 260, 0.04, 3);
612	        if (e.cx > head.cx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx -= 0.3; if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12; }
613	        if (e.cx < head.cx - 250) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.96; e.vx += 0.3; if (e.vx < -12) e.vx = -12; }
614	      }
615	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
616	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
617	      // 升空俯冲:升至头上 200 → 22 速冲玩家
618	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
619	      e.vx *= 0.95;
620	      e.vy -= 0.1;
621	      if (e.vy < -8) e.vy = -8;
622	      if (e.y < head.y - 200 && player) {
623	        e.ai2 = 2;
624	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
625	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
626	        e.vx = dx / d * 22; e.vy = dy / d * 22;
627	      }
628	    } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
629	      // 俯冲到玩家下方/开始上移 → 回环绕(3)
630	      if (!player || e.y > player.y || e.vy < 0) e.ai2 = 3;
631	    } else if (e.ai2 === 5 && player) {
632	      if ((e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) || (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx)) e.ai2 = 0;
633	    }
634	  } else if (style === 34) {
635	    // ---- 激光(NPC 130,:28439-28638) ----
636	    if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
637	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
638	        // 头冲撞中:12 速突进(慢于 2 直接定速,否则阻尼)
639	        if (player) {
640	          const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
641	          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
642	          if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < 2) { e.vx = dx / d * 12; e.vy = dy / d * 12; }
643	          else { e.vx *= 0.97; e.vy *= 0.97; }
644	        }
645	        e.ai3 += 1;
646	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
647	      } else {
648	        e.ai3 += 1;
649	        if (e.ai3 >= 600) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
650	        hoverBand(300, 230, 0.1, 3);
651	        if (e.cx > head.cx + 250) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.94; e.vx -= 0.3; if (e.vx > 9) e.vx = 9; }
652	        if (e.cx < head.cx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.94; e.vx += 0.2; if (e.vx < -8) e.vx = -8; }
653	      }
654	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
655	    } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
656	      // 升空直冲:升至头上 280 → 20 速冲玩家(初 ai3=150 → 首冲提前)
657	      const ax2 = head.cx - 280 * side, ay2 = head.y + 230;
658	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay2 - e.cy, ax2 - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
659	      if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9;
660	      e.vx = (e.vx * 5 + head.vx) / 6;
661	      e.vx += 0.5;
662	      e.vy -= 0.5;
663	      if (e.vy < -9) e.vy = -9;
664	      if (e.y < head.y - 280 && player) {
665	        e.ai2 = 2;
666	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
667	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
668	        e.vx = dx / d * 20; e.vy = dy / d * 20;
669	      }
670	    } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
671	      if (!player || e.y > player.y || e.vy < 0) {
672	        if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai2 = 3; e.ai3 = 0; }
673	        else { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 += 1; }
674	      }
675	    } else if (e.ai2 === 4) {
676	      // 侧向横扫(原版与 5 互切;头冲撞分支偶尔进入)
677	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
678	      e.vy = (e.vy * 5 + head.vy) / 6;
679	      e.vx += 0.5;
680	      if (e.vx > 12) e.vx = 12;
681	      if (player && Math.abs(e.cx - head.cx) > 500) {
682	        e.ai2 = 5;
683	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
684	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
685	        e.vx = dx / d * 17; e.vy = dy / d * 17;
686	      }
687	    } else if (e.ai2 === 5 && player && e.cx < player.cx - 100) {
688	      if (e.ai3 >= 4) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
689	      else { e.ai2 = 4; e.ai3 += 1; }
690	    }
691	  } else if (style === 35) {
692	    // ---- 钳(NPC 128,:28653-28874) ----
693	    if (e.ai2 === 0) {
694	      if (head.ai1 !== 0) {
695	        hoverBand(-100, -100, 0.07, 6);
696	        xWindow(-120 * side, -120 * side, 0.1, 8);
697	      } else {
698	        e.ai3 += 1;
699	        if (e.ai3 >= 1100) { e.ai2 = 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
700	        hoverBand(-150, -150, 0.04, 3);
701	        xWindow(200, 160, 0.2, 8);
702	      }
703	      e.spin = Math.atan2(ay - e.cy, ax - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;

763	/** 原版"逼近目标速度"舵(:28161-28192 锯/激光共用):目标 = dir*spd,0.97 阻尼 ±0.05 步进 */
764	export function seekSteer(e: Enemy, target: { cx: number; cy: number }, spd: number, acc: number): void {
765	  const dx = target.cx - e.cx, dy = target.cy - e.cy;
766	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
767	  const tx = (dx / d) * spd, ty = (dy / d) * spd;
768	  if (e.vx > tx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.97; e.vx -= acc; }
769	  if (e.vx < tx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.97; e.vx += acc; }
770	  if (e.vy > ty) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.97; e.vy -= acc; }
771	  if (e.vy < ty) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.97; e.vy += acc; }
772	}
773	/** 同上但 X/Y 不同步进(钳/炮悬停态,:28817-28848/:29052-29083),0.9 阻尼 */
774	export function seekSteer2(e: Enemy, tx0: number, ty0: number, spd: number, accX: number, accY: number): void {
775	  const dx = tx0 - e.cx, dy = ty0 - e.cy;
776	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
777	  const tx = (dx / d) * spd, ty = (dy / d) * spd;
778	  if (e.vx > tx) { if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.9; e.vx -= accX; }
779	  if (e.vx < tx) { if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.9; e.vx += accX; }
780	  if (e.vy > ty) { if (e.vy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9; e.vy -= accY; }
781	  if (e.vy < ty) { if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.9; e.vy += accY; }
782	}

784	/** AI_037 毁灭者 134-136(NPC.cs:50129-50624):头穿地飞行——

786	 *  白天/玩家死:加速下坠,过岩石层整链消散;体节 135 探针随机激光 100。
787	 *  段链 80 段(原版 GetDestroyerSegmentsCount;链距 44px)由头驱动收缩跟随。 */
788	export function destroyerAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
789	  if (!e.bInit) {
790	    e.bInit = true;

797	  let buried = false;

799	  const ty0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(e.y / TILE) - 1), ty1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE) + 2);
800	  for (let x = tx0; x <= tx1 && !buried; x++) {
801	    for (let y = ty0; y <= ty1; y++) {
802	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
803	      if ((st.flags[i] && st.isSolid(x, y)) || st.liquid[i] > 64) {
804	        if (e.x + e.w > x * TILE && e.x < x * TILE + 16 && e.y + e.h > y * TILE && e.y < y * TILE + 16) { buried = true; break; }
805	      }
806	    }
807	  }
808	
809	  // 白天/玩家死:下坠离场;过岩石层整链消散(:50337-50356)
810	  const dayFlee = game.world.clock.isDay || !player || player.dead;
811	  if (dayFlee) {
812	    e.vy += 1;
813	    if (e.y > game.world.groundLevel * TILE) e.vy += 1;
814	    e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
815	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
816	    driveChain(e, 44);
817	    if (e.y > game.world.rockLevel * TILE) {
818	      for (let s: Enemy | null = e; s; s = s.wormNext) s.dead = true;
819	    }
820	    return;
821	  }
822	
823	  const spd = 16, acc = 0.1, acc2 = 0.15;
824	  if (!buried) {
825	    // 空中(:50416-50458):下坠 0.15 钳 16;慢速时横漂,满速下坠时向玩家 X 修正
826	    e.vy += 0.15;
827	    if (e.vy > spd) e.vy = spd;
828	    const px = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE) * TILE, py = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE) * TILE;
829	    const sx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE) * TILE, sy = Math.floor(e.cy / TILE) * TILE;
830	    const dx = px - sx, dy = py - sy;
831	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.4) {
832	      e.vx += (e.vx < 0 ? -acc * 1.1 : acc * 1.1);
833	    } else if (e.vy === spd) {
834	      if (e.vx < dx) e.vx += acc;
835	      else if (e.vx > dx) e.vx -= acc;
836	    } else if (e.vy > 4) {
837	      e.vx += (e.vx < 0 ? acc * 0.9 : -acc * 0.9);
838	    }
839	    void dy;
840	  } else {
841	    // 地下(:50459-50585):目标速度 = 方向*16,分档加速(同向双轴 0.15→同向单轴 0.10+
842	    // 反轴轻推→主轴 0.11 次轴补速;含防卡死角交叉助推)
843	    const dx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(e.cx / TILE) * TILE;
844	    const dy = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE) * TILE - Math.floor(e.cy / TILE) * TILE;
845	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
846	    const tvx = (dx / d) * spd, tvy = (dy / d) * spd;
847	    const sameX = (e.vx > 0 && tvx > 0) || (e.vx < 0 && tvx < 0);
848	    const sameY = (e.vy > 0 && tvy > 0) || (e.vy < 0 && tvy < 0);
849	    if (sameX && sameY) {
850	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc2; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc2;
851	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc2; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc2;
852	    }
853	    if (sameX || sameY) {
854	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc;
855	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc;
856	      if (Math.abs(tvy) < spd * 0.2 && ((e.vx > 0 && tvx < 0) || (e.vx < 0 && tvx > 0))) {
857	        e.vy += (e.vy > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2);
858	      }
859	      if (Math.abs(tvx) < spd * 0.2 && ((e.vy > 0 && tvy < 0) || (e.vy < 0 && tvy > 0))) {
860	        e.vx += (e.vx > 0 ? acc * 2 : -acc * 2);
861	      }
862	    } else if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
863	      if (e.vx < tvx) e.vx += acc * 1.1; else if (e.vx > tvx) e.vx -= acc * 1.1;
864	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.5) e.vy += (e.vy > 0 ? acc : -acc);
865	    } else {
866	      if (e.vy < tvy) e.vy += acc * 1.1; else if (e.vy > tvy) e.vy -= acc * 1.1;
867	      if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < spd * 0.5) e.vx += (e.vx > 0 ? acc : -acc);
868	    }
869	  }
870	  e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
871	  e.x += e.vx;
872	  e.y += e.vy;
873	  // 段链跟随(linkDist 44 = 44*scale;原版 num24) + 探针弹幕
874	  driveChain(e, 44);
875	  if (!buried) {
876	    for (let s = e.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
877	      if (s.vanillaId !== 135 || s.dead) continue;
878	      // 原版 135:localAI[0] += Next(4),达 Next(1400,26000) 触发(期望 ~6850t/节);
879	      // 近似为每 tick 2/6850 概率 + 视线检查
880	      if (Math.random() < 2 / 6850 && losClear(game, s.cx, s.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) {
881	        const dx = player.cx - s.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40;
882	        const dy = player.cy - s.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40;
883	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
884	        shoot(game, s.cx + dx / d * 5, s.cy + dy / d * 5,

886	      }
887	    }
888	  }
889	}
890	
891	/** 生成蠕虫段链(Enemy.spawnWormChain 的外部包装;Destroyer 用 135/136) */
892	export function enemySpawnChain(head: Enemy, count: number): Enemy[] {
893	  const Ctor = enemyCtor;
894	  if (!Ctor) return [];
895	  return Ctor.spawnWormChain(head, count);
896	}
897	/** 头驱动段链收缩跟随(原版 :50374-50414,linkDist 可配) */
898	export function driveChain(head: Enemy, linkDist: number): void {
899	  for (let s = head.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
900	    const fx = s.wormFollow!;
901	    const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
902	    const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
903	    const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
904	    if (dist > 0.01) {
905	      const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
906	      s.x += dxC * shrink;
907	      s.y += dyC * shrink;
908	      s.spin = Math.atan2(dyC, dxC) + Math.PI / 2;
909	      s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;
910	    }
911	  }
912	}

924	  } else {

926	  }

929	    e.y = (ty2 + 1) * TILE;
930	    e.vy = 0;
931	  }
932	  const tx2 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
933	  if (st.inBounds(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx > 0) {

18	 *  猪鲨370/月总核心398/异教徒439/光皇636/史莱姆皇后657 */
19	const VANILLA_BOSS_IDS = new Set([4, 13, 35, 50, 68, 113, 125, 126, 127, 134, 222, 245, 262, 266, 370, 398, 439, 636, 657]);

24	  width: 16, height: 16, mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
25	  hitSound: ['NPC_Hit_1'], killedSound: ['NPC_Killed_1'], drops: [],
26	};
27	
28	export class Enemy extends Entity {
29	  /** 原版 NPC id（数据驱动路径启用时非空） */
30	  vanillaId: number | null = null;
31	  vanilla: VanillaNpc | null = null;
32	  // ---- 蠕虫多段体（AI_006，NPC.cs:18046）：头 aiStyle 6，编号约定 头+1=身 头+2=尾 ----
33	  /** 链上紧随本段的一段（头 → 身×n → 尾） */
34	  wormNext: Enemy | null = null;
35	  /** 本段跟随的前一段（非空 = 本段是身体段，跳过 AI 只做跟随） */
36	  wormFollow: Enemy | null = null;
37	  /** 上一 tick 位置（段跟随用：段复制前一段的旧位置 = 经典贪吃蛇链） */
38	  prevX = 0; prevY = 0;
39	
40	  /** AI_006 头部（L18645 通用常数 maxSpd=8 accel=0.07；穿墙直行；段链跟随） */
41	  private wormAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
42	    const maxSpd = 8, accel = 0.07;
43	    // 朝向：有玩家朝玩家，无玩家缓慢巡游
44	    let dx: number, dy: number;
45	    if (player) { dx = player.cx - this.cx; dy = player.cy - this.cy; }
46	    else { dx = Math.cos(this.aiT * 0.02) * 10; dy = Math.sin(this.aiT * 0.013) * 10; }
47	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
48	    this.vx += (dx / d) * accel;
49	    this.vy += (dy / d) * accel;
50	    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
51	    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
52	    this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
53	    // 蠕虫穿墙：直接位移（原版 noTileCollide）
54	    this.x += this.vx;
55	    this.y += this.vy;
56	    // 段链跟随（原版 L52271-52308）：方向向量收缩维持 linkDist 间距——
57	    // shrink = (dist - linkDist)/dist；position += dxC*shrink（原版 num63/num64）
58	    for (let s = this.wormNext; s; s = s.wormNext) {
59	      const fx = s.wormFollow!;
60	      const dxC = fx.cx - s.cx;
61	      const dyC = fx.cy - s.cy;
62	      const dist = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
63	      if (dist > 0.01) {
64	        const linkDist = s.w;               // 原版 num64 = width
65	        const shrink = (dist - linkDist) / dist;
66	        s.x += dxC * shrink;
67	        s.y += dyC * shrink;
68	        s.facing = dxC < 0 ? 1 : -1;         // 原版 spriteDirection（L52305）
69	      }
70	    }
71	  }
72	
73	  /** 由头生成段链（原版各 worm 的 NewNPC 链，NPC.cs:18174+）：body×n + tail */
74	  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
75	    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
76	    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
77	    let prev = head;
78	    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
79	      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
80	      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
81	      if (!s) continue;
82	      s.wormFollow = prev;
83	      prev.wormNext = s;
84	      prev = s;
85	      segs.push(s);
86	    }
87	    return segs;
88	  }
89	
90	
91	  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
92	  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
93	    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
94	    if (!v) return null;
95	    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
96	    e.vanillaId = id;
97	    e.vanilla = v;
98	    const hit = vanillaSoundName(v.HitSound) ?? 'NPC_Hit_1';
99	    const kill = vanillaSoundName(v.DeathSound) ?? 'NPC_Killed_1';
100	    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
101	    e.def = {
102	      ...e.def,
103	      name: v.name, hp: v.lifeMax, damage: v.damage, defense: v.defense,
104	      // 原版 knockBackResist 是"承受击退的比例"（0.5=吃一半）；本仓库语义是
105	      // "抗性"（hurt(): resist<0.9 才生效，kbx*(1-resist)）→ 换算 1-比例
106	      knockbackResist: Math.max(0, Math.min(0.89, 1 - (v.knockBackResist ?? 0.5))),
107	      width: v.width, height: v.height, flying,
108	      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),
109	      nightOnly: v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5, underground: false,
110	      mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
111	      hitSound: [hit], killedSound: [kill], drops: v.critter ? [] : vanillaNpcDrops(id),
112	      // 小动物：无接触伤害、不夜行
113	      ...(v.critter ? { damage: 0, nightOnly: false } : {}),
114	    };
115	    e.hp = v.lifeMax;
116	    e.maxHp = v.lifeMax;
117	    e.w = v.width;
118	    e.h = v.height;
119	    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
120	    // EoW 族 alpha=255 = 出生全透明渐显标记（其余 alpha 为静态不透明度,勿动）：

16	/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
17	 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
18	export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
19	  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
20	  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
21	  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
22	    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
23	    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
24	    let key: string | null = null;
25	    if (meta?.key) {
26	      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
27	      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
28	      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
29	      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
30	      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
31	      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
32	    }
33	    map.set(itemId, key);
34	    return key;
35	  };
36	})();
37	
38	/** npc id → 本仓库可用的掉落表（{item: key, chance, min, max}[]；未注册物品过滤）。

41	const _dropsCache = new Map<number, Array<{ item: string; min: number; max: number; chance: number }>>();
42	export function vanillaNpcDrops(id: number): Array<{ item: string; min: number; max: number; chance: number }> {
43	  const hit = _dropsCache.get(id);
44	  if (hit) return hit;
45	  const raw = VANILLA_NPC_LOOT[String(id)];
46	  const out: Array<{ item: string; min: number; max: number; chance: number }> = [];
47	  if (raw) {
48	    for (const d of raw) {
49	      const key = vanillaItemKey(d.item);
50	      if (key) out.push({ item: key, min: d.min, max: d.max, chance: d.chance });
51	    }
52	  }
53	  _dropsCache.set(id, out);
54	  return out;
55	}

1	// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
2	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import type { World } from '../world/World';
4	
5	export interface Body {
6	  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
7	  w: number; h: number;
8	  vx: number; vy: number;
9	  onGround: boolean;
10	  hitWall: boolean;
11	  hitHead: boolean;
12	  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
13	  dropThrough?: boolean;
14	}
15	
16	/** 格子的半砖碰撞盒（原版 Collision.cs:1320-1324 三处一致）：下半 8px。
17	 *  返回 null = 非实心；[top, bottom] = 碰撞盒的像素 y 区间 */
18	function solidSpan(world: World, tx: number, ty: number): [number, number] | null {
19	  const st = world.store;
20	  if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return null;
21	  const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[st.idx(tx, ty)] ? 8 : 0);
22	  return [top, (ty + 1) * TILE];
23	}
24	
25	/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */
26	function moveAxis(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number): { hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
27	  let hitX = false, hitY = false;
28	  // X 轴
29	  if (dx !== 0) {
30	    b.x += dx;
31	    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
32	    const edgeX = dir > 0 ? b.x + b.w : b.x;
33	    const tx = Math.floor(edgeX / TILE);
34	    const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
35	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
36	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
37	      // 半砖只占下半：身体底部没超过半砖顶面（ty*16+8）则不拦
38	      if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0]) {
39	        if (dir > 0) b.x = tx * TILE - b.w;
40	        else b.x = (tx + 1) * TILE;
41	        b.vx = 0;
42	        hitX = true;
43	        break;
44	      }
45	    }
46	  }
47	  // Y 轴
48	  if (dy !== 0) {
49	    b.y += dy;
50	    const dir = Math.sign(dy);
51	    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? b.y + b.h : b.y;
52	    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
53	    const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
54	    // 原版取整行最高面（Collision.cs:1610-1631 num13 取最大盒顶），不能 break 在最左列——
55	    // 否则左列半砖/右列整砖时身体会嵌进整砖 8px
56	    let bestTop = Infinity;
57	    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
58	      const solid = world.store.isSolid(tx, ty);
59	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
60	      const plat = !b.dropThrough && dir > 0 && world.store.isPlatform(tx, ty)
61	        && (b.y + b.h) - dy <= ty * TILE + 1; // 上一位置在平台之上
62	      if (dir > 0) {
63	        if (!solid && !plat) continue;
64	        // 落地门槛（Collision.cs:1610/1631）：新底部越过盒顶 且 上一位置在盒顶之上，
65	        // 否则（嵌入/侧入）不吸附——半砖盒顶是 ty*16+8，只进入行上半不算落地
66	        const top = span ? span[0] : ty * TILE;
67	        if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;
68	        if (top < bestTop) bestTop = top;
69	      } else {
70	        if (!solid) continue;
71	        // 上顶：上一位置在盒底之下才命中；取最低盒底（最先撞到的天花板）
72	        const bottom = (ty + 1) * TILE;
73	        if (b.y >= bottom || b.y - dy < bottom - 0.01) continue;
74	        if (bottom < bestTop) bestTop = bottom;
75	      }
76	    }
77	    if (bestTop !== Infinity) {
78	      if (dir > 0) { b.y = bestTop - b.h; b.onGround = true; }
79	      else { b.y = bestTop; b.hitHead = true; }
80	      b.vy = 0;
81	      hitY = true;
82	    }
83	  }
84	  return { hitX, hitY };
85	}
86	
87	/** 移动并碰撞。高速时分子步防穿透。 */
88	export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {
89	  b.onGround = false;
90	  b.hitWall = false;
91	  b.hitHead = false;
92	  const maxStep = 8;
93	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) / maxStep));
94	  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
95	    const sx = dx / steps, sy = dy / steps;
96	    const { hitX, hitY } = moveAxis(b, world, sx, sy);
97	    if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }
98	    if (hitY) dy = 0;
99	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) break;
100	  }
101	}
102

2	import type { GameHooks } from './types';

5	
6	export abstract class Entity {
7	  id = 0;
8	  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
9	  w = 16; h = 16;
10	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
11	  dead = false;
12	  onGround = false;
13	  hitWall = false;
14	  hitHead = false;
15	  dropThrough = false;
16	
17	  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
18	  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;
19	
20	  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
21	  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
22	  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
23	    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
28	function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
29	  let w = 0;
30	  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
31	    const e = list[r];
32	    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
33	  }
34	  list.length = w;
35	}
36	
37	export class EntityManager {
38	  nextId = 1;
39	  enemies: Entity[] = [];
40	  drops: Entity[] = [];
41	  npcs: Entity[] = [];
42	  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
43	  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
44	  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）
45	
46	  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
47	    e.id = this.nextId++;
48	    this[bucket].push(e);
49	  }
50	
51	  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
52	    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
53	    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
54	      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
55	      compact(list);
56	    }
57	  }
58	
59	  clear() {
60	    this.enemies.length = 0;
61	    this.drops.length = 0;
62	    this.npcs.length = 0;
63	    this.projectiles.length = 0;
64	    this.props.length = 0;
65	    this.critters.length = 0;
66	  }
67	
68	  all(): Entity[] {
69	    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
70	  }
71	}

};

```
export function planteraAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const l = la(e);
  // flag50 = 暴怒(玩家非丛林/高于地表/低于地狱) flag51 = 玩家死(:31875-31884)
  const flag51 = !player || player.dead;
  const flag50 = false;  // 暴怒分支省略(:31923-31928):ZoneJungle/UnderworldLayer 群系判定暂缺
  if (!e.bInit) {
    e.bInit = true;
    l[0] = 1;
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) spawnPart(game, e, 263);   // :31895-31897 钩蔓×3
  }
  // 钩蔓(263, aiStyle 52)前 3 只的中心(:31898-31913)
  let sumX = 0, sumY = 0, cnt = 0;
  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
    if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 52) {
      sumX += n.cx; sumY += n.cy; cnt++;
      if (cnt > 2) break;   // 原版 num791 > 2 break(取 3 只)
    }
  }
  let avgX = cnt > 0 ? sumX / cnt : e.cx;   // 原版除以 0 会 NaN;无钩蔓时退化为本体位(防漂移)
  let avgY = cnt > 0 ? sumY / cnt : e.cy;
  // 速度/加速度按阶段(:31914-31928)
  let num793 = 2.5, num794 = 0.025;
  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) { num793 = 5; num794 = 0.05; }
  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num793 = 7;
  // 暴怒:num793 += 8 / num794 = 0.15(省略)
  let num795 = player.cx - avgX, num796 = player.cy - avgY;
  if (flag51) { num795 = -num795; num796 = -num796; num793 += 8; }

```
export function planteraTentacleAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  const l = la(e);
  const st = game.world.store;
  const boss = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 262) ?? null;
  if (!boss) { e.hp -= 9999; if (e.hp <= 0) e.dead = true; return; }  // :32241-32246 StrikeNPCNoInteraction
  const dead = !player || player.dead;   // flag54: Main.player[boss.target].dead
  // flag53 = 暴怒(玩家离丛林/地表/地狱层) localAI[0] -= 4 → 省略(:32247-32256)
  if (e.ai0 === 0 || e.ai1 === 0) l[0] = 0;     // :32268-32272
  l[0] -= 1;
  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) l[0] -= 2;      // :32275-32277
  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) l[0] -= 2;
  // if (flag53) l[0] -= 6;  (暴怒省略)
  if (!dead && l[0] <= 0 && e.ai0 !== 0) {
    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
      if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === e.vanillaId && (n.vx !== 0 || n.vy !== 0)) { l[0] = rndRange(60, 301); break; }
    }
  }
  if (l[0] <= 0) {
    l[0] = rndRange(300, 600);
    // 搜索锚点 tile(:32284-32350)
    let found = false, tries = 0;
    while (!found && tries <= 1000) {
      tries++;
      let tx0 = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), ty0 = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
      if (e.ai0 === 0) { tx0 = Math.floor((player.cx + boss.cx) / 2 / TILE); ty0 = Math.floor((player.cy + boss.cy) / 2 / TILE); }
      if (dead) { tx0 = Math.floor(boss.x / TILE); ty0 = Math.floor((boss.y + 400) / TILE); }
      const rad = 20 + Math.floor(100 * (tries / 1000));
      let cx = tx0 + rndRange(-rad, rad + 1), cy = ty0 + rndRange(-rad, rad + 1);
      if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2 && rnd(6) === 0) {
        const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
        if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.wall[st.idx(ptx, pty)] > 0) { cx = ptx; cy = pty; }
      }
      if (st.inBounds(cx, cy) && (st.isSolid(cx, cy) || (st.wall[st.idx(cx, cy)] > 0 && (tries > 500 || boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2)))) {
        found = true; e.ai0 = cx; e.ai1 = cy;
      }
    }
  }
  if (!(e.ai0 > 0) || !(e.ai1 > 0)) return;   // :32352-32355
  let num834 = 6;
  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) num834 = 8;
  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) num834 = 10;
  if (dead) num834 *= 2;   // flag54(玩家死)离场加速
  const ax = e.ai0 * TILE - 8, ay = e.ai1 * TILE - 8;
  const dx = ax - e.cx, dy = ay - e.cy;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (d < 12 + num834) { e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy; }
  else { const s = num834 / d; e.vx = dx * s; e.vy = dy * s; }
  e.spin = Math.atan2(boss.cy - e.cy, boss.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;   // :32396-32405
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}
```

 "content": "// 蜂后 222(aiStyle 43,NPC.cs:30312-30998)+ 世纪之花族 1:1 移植(Terarria1456,1.4.5.6)。\n//   aiStyle 50(NPC.cs:31807-31872)= NPC 261 真菌孢子 / 265 孢子(漂移族,261 撞墙即碎)\n//   aiStyle 51(NPC.cs:31873-32237)= NPC 262 世纪之花本体(钩蔓均值牵引 + 二阶段触须)\n//   aiStyle 52(NPC.cs:32238-32405)= NPC 263 世纪之花钩蔓(抓墙锚定,牵引本体)\n// 经典模式参数 1:1;expert/getGoodWorld 分支只注释保留;netcode(netUpdate/Main.netMode)跳过。\n// 本体/部件均 noTileCollide(原版 SetDefaults),故位移由各核自行积分(e.x+=vx)。\nimport type { Enemy } from './Enemy';\nimport type { GameHooks } from './types';\nimport type { Player } from './Player';\nimport { TILE } from '../core/constants';\nimport { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';\nimport { spawnPart, shoot, accelTo, losClear } from './bossAI';\n\n/** 原版 localAI[0..3](Enemy 的 ai0-ai3 对应 ai[],localAI 是独立数组 → WeakMap 落盘) */\nconst localAI = new WeakMap<Enemy, number[]>();\nconst la = (e: Enemy): number[] => {\n  let a = localAI.get(e);\n  if (!a) { a = [0, 0, 0, 0]; localAI.set(e, a); }\n  return a;\n};\n\n/** Main.rand.Next(n) → [0,n) */\nconst rnd = (n: number): number => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);\n/** Main.rand.Next(a, b) → [a,b) */\nconst rndRange = (a: number, b: number): number => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a));\nconst clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);\n\n// ============================================================================\n// AI_043 蜂后 222(NPC.cs:30312-30998)\n// ai[0] 状态:-1 选下一招 / 0 悬浮-俯冲循环 / 2 飞至玩家上方 / 1 机枪蜜蜂 /\n//            3 毒刺弹幕 / 4 远距追赶 / 5 玩家死离场。\n// ai[1]:状态 0 内的半程计数(偶=悬浮 奇=冲撞减速,>2*num662 换招);离开时暂存上一状态供 -1 去重。\n// ai[2]:状态 0 减速门(1=正在减速);状态 1/3 已发弹计数。\n// ============================================================================\nexport function queenBeeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  // 首帧复位(ai0 默认 -1120 是史莱姆族约定,原版 ai[0] 从 0 起步)\n  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; la(e)[0] = 0; }\n  // 玩家失效 = 原版 TargetClosest 落空后的 dead4(:30316-30319)\n  const dead4 = !player || player.dead;\n  // 暴怒度 num658(:30321-30327):地表 +1 / 非丛林 +1(getGoodWorld +0.5 省略)。\n  // ZoneJungle 群系判定暂缺 → 只保留 worldSurface 一档(见汇报简化项)\n  let num658 = 0;\n  if (e.y / TILE < game.world.groundLevel) num658 += 1;\n  const num659 = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) : 1e9;\n  // timeLeft 维持 ≥60(:30330-30334)由共享 Despawn 系统承担,不在此重复\n  if (e.ai0 !== 5 && num659 > 3000) e.ai0 = 4;   // :30336-30341 远距追赶\n  if (dead4) e.ai0 = 5;                           // :30342-30345\n\n  let direction = e.facing || 1;\n  if (e.ai0 === 5) {\n    // 离场(:30346-30383):朝世界远端飞,vx 恒向远端加速 0.08\n    e.vy *= 0.98;\n    direction = e.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;\n    e.facing = direction;\n    const halfW = game.world.store.w * TILE / 2;   // Main.maxTilesX * 8 = 世界中线\n    if (e.x < halfW) {\n      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98; else la(e)[0] = 1;\n      e.vx -= 0.08;\n    } else {\n      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98; else la(e)[0] = 1;\n      e.vx += 0.08;\n    }\n    // EncourageDespawn(10):共享 Despawn 系统兜底\n  } else if (e.ai0 === -1) {\n    // 选下一招(:30384-30408):{0,2,3} 随机,避开 ai1 暂存的上一状态\n    let pick: number;\n    do {\n      pick = rnd(3);\n      if (pick === 1) pick = 2; else if (pick === 2) pick = 3;\n    } while (pick === e.ai1);\n    e.ai0 = pick;\n    e.ai1 = 0;\n    e.ai2 = 0;\n  } else if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n    // 悬浮-俯冲循环(:30409-30605)\n    const num662 = 2;   // :30409-30417(经典 2;expert 随血量 +1/3/5 档)\n    if (e.ai1 > 2 * num662 && e.ai1 % 2 === 0) {\n      // 循环数满 → 换招(:30419-30427);ai1 归 0 = 上一状态是 0\n      e.ai0 = -1;\n      e.ai1 = 0;\n      e.ai2 = 0;\n    } else if (e.ai1 % 2 === 0) {\n      // 悬浮/俯冲判定(:30428-30499)\n      direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;\n      const num663 = 20 + 20 * num658;\n      if (Math.abs(e.cy - player!.cy) < num663) {\n        // 俯冲(:30451-30499):localAI[0]=1(冲刺动画),12(+7*num658) 速直冲玩家\n        la(e)[0] = 1;\n        e.ai1 += 1;\n        e.ai2 = 0;\n        const num664 = 12 + 7 * num658;   // expert 随血量 16→24 省略\n        const dx = player!.cx - e.cx, dy = player!.cy - e.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        e.vx = (dx / d) * num664;\n        e.vy = (dy / d) * num664;\n        e.facing = direction;\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.5);   // SoundEngine.PlaySound(29, ..., 125) 蜂后冲刺吼,近似\n      } else {\n        // 悬浮(:30500-30565):Y 朝玩家 0.15(+0.5*num658) 钳 ±12(+3*num658);\n        // X 三段 >600 加速 / <300 反向 / 中带阻尼 0.8,钳 ±16\n        la(e)[0] = 0;\n        const num668 = 12 + 3 * num658;\n        const num669 = 0.15 + 0.5 * num658;\n        e.vy += e.cy < player!.cy ? num669 : -num669;\n        e.vy = clamp(e.vy, -num668, num668);\n        const dxAbs = Math.abs(e.cx - player!.cx);\n        if (dxAbs > 600) e.vx += 0.15 * direction;\n        else if (dxAbs < 300) e.vx -= 0.15 * direction;\n        else e.vx *= 0.8;\n        e.vx = clamp(e.vx, -16, 16);\n        e.facing = direction;\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 俯冲后(:30566-30605)\n      direction = e.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;\n      e.facing = direction;\n      const num670 = 600 - Math.floor(100 * num658);\n      const num671 = e.cx < player!.cx ? -1 : 1;\n      let flag45 = false;\n      // 冲过头/垂直脱靶 → 进入减速门 ai2=1\n      if (direction === num671 && Math.abs(e.cx - player!.cx) > num670) { e.ai2 = 1; flag45 = true; }\n      if (Math.abs(e.cy - player!.cy) > num670 * 1.5) { e.ai2 = 1; flag45 = true; }\n      if (num658 > 0 && flag45) { e.vx *= 0.5; e.vy *= 0.5; }\n      if (e.ai2 === 1) {\n        // 减速(:30600-30613 主体):0.9/tick(num658>0 再 0.7),速降 <0.1 → 回悬浮(ai1+1)\n        la(e)[0] = 0;\n        e.vx *= 0.9;\n        e.vy *= 0.9;\n        if (num658 > 0) { e.vx *= 0.7; e.vy *= 0.7; }\n        if (Math.abs(e.vx) + Math.abs(e.vy) < 0.1) {\n          e.ai2 = 0;\n          e.ai1 += 1;\n        }\n      } else {\n        la(e)[0] = 1;   // 仍朝目标飞行,维持冲刺动画\n      }\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {\n    // 飞至玩家上方 200(:30606-30690):12 速 / 0.07 步进;距目标 <200 → 机枪蜜蜂\n    direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;\n    e.facing = direction;\n    const num675 = player!.cx - e.cx;\n    const num676 = player!.cy - 200 - e.cy;\n    const num677 = Math.hypot(num675, num676);\n    if (num677 < 200) {\n      e.ai0 = 1;\n      e.ai1 = 0;\n    } else {\n      const t = 12 / num677;\n      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, num675 * t, 0.07);\n      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, num676 * t, 0.07);\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {\n    // 机枪蜜蜂(:30691-30830)\n    la(e)[0] = 0;\n    direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;\n    // 毒刺口 vector78(:30697):x 偏 rand(20)*direction,y 取 0.8h\n    const vx78 = e.x + e.w / 2 + rnd(20) * direction;\n    const vy78 = e.y + e.h * 0.8;\n    const distToPlayer = Math.hypot(player!.cx - e.cx, player!.cy - e.cy);\n    e.ai1 += 1;   // expert:邻域玩家数/血量档加成省略(:30713-30736)\n    const num683 = Math.floor(40 - 18 * num658);\n    let flag46 = false;\n    if (e.ai1 > num683) {\n      e.ai1 = 0;\n      e.ai2 += 1;\n      flag46 = true;\n    }\n    if (flag46 && losClear(game, vx78, vy78, player!.cx, player!.cy)) {\n      // 出蜂(:30752-30778):210/211 小蜂,5 速朝玩家\n      const bee = spawnPart(game, e, rndRange(210, 212));\n      if (bee) {\n        const dx = player!.cx - e.cx, dy = player!.cy - e.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        bee.vx = (dx / d) * 5;\n        bee.vy = (dy / d) * 5;\n        // 原版 localAI[0]=60(蜜蜂寿命锚);本框架 swarmerAI 无该语义,Despawn 系统兜底\n      }\n      game.playSfx('hit', 0.4);   // SoundID 3(NPCHit 系)近似\n    }\n    if (distToPlayer > 400 || !losClear(game, vx78, vy78 - 30, player!.cx, player!.cy)) {\n      // 拉近(:30779-30823):以毒刺口为基准 14 速 / 0.1 步进\n      const dx = player!.cx - vx78, dy = player!.cy - vy78;\n      const t = 14 / (Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1);\n      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, dx * t, 0.1);\n      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, dy * t, 0.1);\n    } else {\n      e.vx *= 0.9;\n      e.vy *= 0.9;\n    }\n    e.facing = direction;\n    if (e.ai2 > 5) {\n      // 6 波蜂后换招(:30824-30829);ai1=1 供 -1 去重(1 不在 {0,2,3} 内,等效不去重)\n      e.ai0 = -1;\n      e.ai1 = 1;\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai0 === 3) {\n    // 毒刺弹幕(:30831-30981)\n    const num688 = 4 + 6 * num658;\n    const num689 = 0.05 + 0.2 * num658;\n    direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;\n    const vx80 = e.x + e.w / 2 + rnd(20) * direction;\n    const vy80 = e.y + e.h * 0.8;\n    const num690 = player!.cx - e.cx;\n    const num691 = player!.cy - 300 - e.cy;\n    let num692 = Math.hypot(num690, num691);\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    const num693 = 40 - Math.floor(5 * num658);   // expert 血量档 15/25/30/35 省略\n    // 每隔 num693 tick 且位于玩家上方且视线通 → 毒刺 719\n    if (e.ai1 % num693 === num693 - 1 && e.y + e.h < player!.y\n      && losClear(game, vx80, vy80, player!.cx, player!.cy)) {\n      const num694 = 8 + 7 * num658;               // expert +2/+5 省略\n      const num695 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(80 - 39 * num658));\n      const num696 = Math.max(1, Math.floor(40 - 19 * num658));\n      let dx = player!.cx - vx80 + rndRange(-num695, num695 + 1);\n      let dy = player!.cy - vy80 + rndRange(-num696, num696 + 1);\n      const t = num694 / (Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1);\n      dx *= t;\n      dy *= t;\n      shoot(game, vx80, vy80, dx, dy, 11, 719, 300);   // 伤害 11 / timeLeft 300(:30875-30889)\n      game.playSfx('hit', 0.4);                        // SoundID.Item17 近似\n    }\n    if (!losClear(game, vx80, vy80 - 30, player!.cx, player!.cy)) {\n      // 隔墙:直接朝玩家 14 速 / 0.1 步进(:30890-30934)\n      const dx = player!.cx - vx80, dy = player!.cy - vy80;\n      const t = 14 / (Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1);\n      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, dx * t, 0.1);\n      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, dy * t, 0.1);\n    } else if (num692 > 100) {\n      // 朝悬浮点(玩家上方 300)逼近 4 速 / 0.05 步进,符号翻转双步(:30935-30978)\n      direction = player!.cx < e.cx ? -1 : 1;\n      e.facing = direction;\n      const t = num688 / num692;\n      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, num690 * t, num689);\n      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, num691 * t, num689);\n      // 注:原版此处符号翻转时步进 ×2(velocity.X += num689 * 2f);\n      // accelTo 只单步,差异极小(0.05/tick)且仅反向瞬间,不单独展开\n      void num692;\n    }\n    const num703 = 20 - 5 * num658;\n    if (e.ai1 > num693 * num703) {\n      // 弹幕时长满(40*20=800t)→ 换招(:30979-30981);ai1=3 供 -1 去重\n      e.ai0 = -1;\n      e.ai1 = 3;\n    }\n  } else if (e.ai0 === 4) {\n    // 远距追赶(:30982-30998):14 速混合逼近,进入 2000px → 换招\n    la(e)[0] = 1;\n    let tx = player!.cx - e.cx, ty = player!.cy - e.cy;\n    const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;\n    tx = (tx / d) * 14;\n    ty = (ty / d) * 14;\n    e.vx = (e.vx * 14 + tx) / 15;\n    e.vy = (e.vy * 14 + ty) / 15;\n    direction = e.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1;\n    e.facing = direction;\n    if (num659 < 2000) {\n      e.ai0 = -1;\n      la(e)[0] = 0;\n    }\n  }\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// AI_050 孢子漂移族(NPC.cs:31807-31872):NPC 261 真菌孢子 / 265 孢子(世纪之花二阶段产物)。\n// 弱重力 0.02 下沉 + X 朝玩家 0.1 步进;261 撞墙即碎(life=0),265 穿墙。\n// ============================================================================\nexport function planteraHookAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  // EncourageDespawn(5):共享 Despawn 系统兜底\n  e.vy += 0.02;                                    // :31819\n  if (player) {\n    if (e.vy < 0 && player.y > e.y + 100) e.vy *= 0.95;   // :31821-31824\n    if (e.vy > 1) e.vy = 1;\n    // X 朝玩家 0.1 步进,逆向先 0.98 阻尼(:31829-31860;expert 双档省略)\n    if (e.x + e.w < player.x) {\n      if (e.vx < 0) e.vx *= 0.98;\n      e.vx += 0.1;\n    } else if (e.x > player.x + player.w) {\n      if (e.vx > 0) e.vx *= 0.98;\n      e.vx -= 0.1;\n    }\n  }\n  if (e.vx > 5 || e.vx < -5) e.vx *= 0.97;         // :31861-31864\n  e.spin = e.vx * 0.2;                             // rotation = velocity.X * 0.2f(:31865)\n  if (e.vanillaId === 261) {\n    // 真菌孢子:noTileCollide=false,撞墙即碎(:31810-31818)\n    e.onGround = false; e.hitWall = false; e.hitHead = false;\n    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);\n    if (e.hitWall || e.hitHead || e.onGround) {\n      e.hp = 0;\n      e.dead = true;\n      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#7A9E4F', 5, 1.5);   // HitEffect(0,100) 近似\n    }\n  } else {\n    // 265 孢子 noTileCollide(:31815-31817)\n    e.x += e.vx;\n    e.y += e.vy;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// AI_051 世纪之花 262(NPC.cs:31873-32237)\n// 本体无重力,由钩蔓(263, aiStyle 52)均值牵引:目标点 = 钩蔓中心 + 朝玩家偏移(≤500px);\n// 一阶段(血>50%):防 36 / 接触伤 50,种子弹幕 275(22)/276 毒种(27)/277 刺球(31);\n// 二阶段(血≤50%):防 10 / 接触伤 70,提速(2.5→5→7 / 0.025→0.05),触须 264×8 + 孢子 265。\n// plantBoss 静态引用由\"场上 262 查找\"等价替代(单 Boss 语义)。\n// ============================================================================\nexport function planteraAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  const l = la(e);\n  // 玩家死(:31875-31883) → flag51 = 反向离场\n  const flag51 = !player || player.dead;\n  // 暴怒 flag50(:31923-31928):玩家非丛林/高于地表/低于地狱层 → 省略(见汇报简化项)\n  const flag50 = false;\n  // 初始化(:31886-31892):localAI[0]=1 + 钩蔓 263×3\n  if (!e.bInit) {\n    e.bInit = true;\n    l[0] = 1;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) spawnPart(game, e, 263);\n  }\n  // 钩蔓中心(:31893-31913):场上前 3 只 aiStyle 52 的位置均值\n  let sumX = 0, sumY = 0, cnt = 0;\n  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {\n    if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 52) {\n      sumX += n.cx;\n      sumY += n.cy;\n      cnt++;\n      if (cnt > 2) break;   // 原版 num791 > 2 break(只取 3 只)\n    }\n  }\n  // 原版 num791=0 时除 0 得 NaN(本体漂移);此处退化为本体位保持可玩\n  let num789 = cnt > 0 ? sumX / cnt : e.cx;\n  let num790 = cnt > 0 ? sumY / cnt : e.cy;\n  // 速度/加速度档(:31914-31928):2.5/0.025 → 半血 5/0.05 → 1/4 血 7\n  let num793 = 2.5;\n  let num794 = 0.025;\n  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) { num793 = 5; num794 = 0.05; }\n  if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num793 = 7;\n  // flag50:num793 += 8 / num794 = 0.15;expert:+1 ×1.1 / +0.01 ×1.1 省略(:31930-31941)\n  // 目标点 = 钩蔓中心 + 朝玩家偏移,钳 500px(:31942-31968)\n  let num795 = player!.cx - num789;\n  let num796 = player!.cy - num790;\n  if (flag51) {\n    num795 = -num795;\n    num796 = -num796;\n    num793 += 8;\n  }\n  let num797 = Math.hypot(num795, num796);\n  const num798 = 500 + (flag50 ? 350 : 0);   // expert +150 省略\n  if (num797 >= num798) {\n    const s = num798 / num797;\n    num795 *= s;\n    num796 *= s;\n  }\n  num789 += num795;\n  num790 += num796;\n  // 逼近目标点(:31969-32015):距离 < num793 保持现速,否则归一到 num793;0.025 步进(符号翻转双步)\n  num795 = num789 - e.cx;\n  num796 = num790 - e.cy;\n  num797 = Math.hypot(num795, num796);\n  if (num797 < num793) {\n    num795 = e.vx;\n    num796 = e.vy;\n  } else {\n    const s = num793 / num797;\n    num795 *= s;\n    num796 *= s;\n  }\n  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, num795, num794);\n  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, num796, num794);\n  // 朝向玩家旋转(:32016-32019)\n  e.spin = Math.atan2(player!.cy - e.cy, player!.cx - e.cx) + Math.PI / 2;\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n\n  if (e.hp > e.maxHp / 2) {\n    // ---- 一阶段(:32020-32133) ----\n    e.def.defense = 36;                       // :32021\n    e.def.damage = 50;                        // GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(50) 经典 = 50\n    l[1] += 1;                                // :32034\n    for (const th of [0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6]) if (e.hp < e.maxHp * th) l[1] += 1;\n    // flag50:+3 / expert:+1 / expert justHit 概率蓄力 / getGoodWorld:+1 均省略(:32051-32066)\n    if (l[1] > 80) {\n      l[1] = 0;\n      // 视线检查(:32069-32078):Collision.CanHit(position,w,h,player,...) 近似\n      const flag52 = losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player!.x, player!.y);\n      if (flag52) {\n        // 种子 275 / 毒种 276 / 刺球 277(:32080-32133)\n        const num802 = 15;                    // expert 17 省略\n        let num803 = player!.cx - e.cx;\n        let num804 = player!.cy - e.cy;\n        const t = num802 / (Math.hypot(num803, num804) || 1);\n        num803 *= t;\n        num804 *= t;\n        let num806 = 22;\n        let num807 = 275;\n        let life = 300;\n        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8 && rnd(4) === 0) {\n          num806 = 27;                        // 毒种 276\n          num807 = 276;\n          l[1] = -30;                         // 下发弹延迟 30t\n        } else if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8 && rnd(8) === 0) {\n          num806 = 31;                        // 刺球 277\n          num807 = 277;\n          l[1] = -120;                        // 下发弹延迟 120t\n          life = 1200;                        // 原版不设 timeLeft(保持默认),此处取本框架默认\n        }\n        shoot(game, e.cx + num803 * 3, e.cy + num804 * 3, num803, num804, num806, num807, life);\n      }\n    }\n  } else {\n    // ---- 二阶段(血≤50%,:32134-32237) ----\n    e.def.defense = 10;                       // :32134\n    e.def.damage = 70;                        // GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(70) 经典 = 70\n    // 首次进二阶段:触须 264×8(:32144-32170;expert 追加分配/getGoodWorld +6 省略)\n    if (l[0] === 1) {\n      l[0] = 2;\n      for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) spawnPart(game, e, 264);\n    }\n    // 二阶段入场花瓣 Gore 378/379/380(:32194-32200)→ 粒子近似\n    if (l[2] === 0) {\n      l[2] = 1;\n      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#5FA054', 8, 2);\n    }\n    // expert 每 60t 补触须(:32172-32190)省略\n    l[1] += 1;                                // :32202\n    for (const th of [0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1]) if (e.hp < e.maxHp * th) l[1] += 1;\n    if (l[1] >= 350) {\n      // 孢子 265(:32216-32236):8 速,落点偏移(下方目标去掉 0.2|dx| 弧垂)\n      const num820 = player!.cx - e.cx + rndRange(-10, 11);\n      let num821 = Math.abs(num820 * 0.2);\n      let num822 = player!.cy - e.cy + rndRange(-10, 11);\n      if (num822 > 0) num821 = 0;\n      num822 -= num821;\n      const t = 8 / (Math.hypot(num820, num822) || 1);\n      const spore = spawnPart(game, e, 265);\n      if (spore) {\n        spore.vx = num820 * t;\n        spore.vy = num822 * t;\n      }\n      l[1] = 0;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// AI_052 世纪之花钩蔓 263(NPC.cs:32238-32405):抓墙锚定 + 牵引本体。\n// ai[0]/ai[1] = 锚点 tile 坐标;localAI[0] = 换锚倒计时(每次抓墙后 300-600t,血低加速);\n// 到点后钉在锚点(速度=残差),本体以钩蔓均值+玩家偏移为目标被拉动。\n// plantBoss 静态引用由\"场上 262 查找\"等价替代;本体消失 → 自毁(StrikeNPCNoInteraction 9999)。\n// ============================================================================\nexport function planteraTentacleAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  const l = la(e);\n  const st = game.world.store;\n  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }\n  // 本体引用(原版静态 plantBoss;:32241-32246 本体消失 → 9999 自伤)\n  const boss = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 262) ?? null;\n  if (!boss) {\n    e.hp -= 9999;\n    if (e.hp <= 0) e.dead = true;\n    return;\n  }\n  const target: { cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; dead: boolean } = player ?? boss;\n  const flag54 = !player || player.dead;    // 玩家死(:32248-32250)\n  // 暴怒 flag53(玩家离丛林/地表/地狱层,localAI[0]-4/换锚-6/速度 ×2)省略(见汇报简化项)\n  const flag53 = false;\n  // 换锚倒计时(:32268-32291)\n  if (e.ai0 === 0 || e.ai1 === 0) l[0] = 0;\n  l[0] -= 1;\n  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) l[0] -= 2;   // :32275-32277\n  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) l[0] -= 2;\n  if (flag53) l[0] -= 4;                     // 原版入口 -4(:32252-32256)+ 换锚前 -6(:32283-32285)合并近似\n  // 错峰:其他同类钩蔓仍在飞行 → 随机 60-300t 后再换(:32286-32298)\n  if (!flag54 && l[0] <= 0 && e.ai0 !== 0) {\n    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {\n      if (n !== e && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 263 && (n.vx !== 0 || n.vy !== 0)) {\n        l[0] = rndRange(60, 300);\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  if (l[0] <= 0) {\n    // 搜新锚点(:32299-32351):玩家附近(无锚时取玩家-本体中点)随机扩散找实心/墙 tile\n    l[0] = rndRange(300, 600);\n    let found = false;\n    let num826 = 0;\n    while (!found && num826 <= 1000) {\n      num826++;\n      let num827 = Math.floor(target.cx / TILE);\n      let num828 = Math.floor(target.cy / TILE);\n      if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n        // 首锚:取玩家与本体中点(:32309-32312)\n        num827 = Math.floor((target.cx + boss.cx) / 32);\n        num828 = Math.floor((target.cy + boss.cy) / 32);\n      }\n      if (flag54) {\n        num827 = Math.floor(boss.x / TILE);\n        num828 = Math.floor((boss.y + 400) / TILE);\n      }\n      const num829 = 20 + Math.floor(100 * (num826 / 1000));   // 扩散半径 20→120\n      let num830 = num827 + rndRange(-num829, num829 + 1);\n      let num831 = num828 + rndRange(-num829, num829 + 1);\n      // 半血后 1/6 概率直接试玩家所在格(有墙才用,:32322-32332)\n      if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2 && rnd(6) === 0) {\n        const ptx = Math.floor(target.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(target.cy / TILE);\n        if (st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.wall[st.idx(ptx, pty)] > 0) {\n          num830 = ptx;\n          num831 = pty;\n        }\n      }\n      // 实心 tile / (墙 tile 且搜索过半或本体半血)(:32333-32350)\n      if (st.inBounds(num830, num831)\n        && (st.isSolid(num830, num831)\n          || (st.wall[st.idx(num830, num831)] > 0 && (num826 > 500 || boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2)))) {\n        found = true;\n        e.ai0 = num830;\n        e.ai1 = num831;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  if (!(e.ai0 > 0) || !(e.ai1 > 0)) return;   // :32352-32355 无锚不动\n  // 收线速度(:32356-32375):6 → 本体半血 8 → 1/4 血 10(expert +1/+1、暴怒/玩家死 ×2)\n  let num834 = 6;\n  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 2) num834 = 8;\n  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp / 4) num834 = 10;\n  if (flag54) num834 *= 2;\n  // 朝锚点(锚点 tile 中心 - 8px)收线(:32383-32395):近于 12+速 → 速度=残差(钉住)\n  const num835 = e.ai0 * TILE - 8 - e.cx;\n  const num836 = e.ai1 * TILE - 8 - e.cy;\n  const num837 = Math.hypot(num835, num836);\n  if (num837 < 12 + num834) {\n    e.vx = num835;\n    e.vy = num836;\n  } else {\n    const s = num834 / num837;\n    e.vx = num835 * s;\n    e.vy = num836 * s;\n  }\n  // 朝本体旋转(:32396-32405)\n  e.spin = Math.atan2(boss.cy - e.cy, boss.cx - e.cx) - Math.PI / 2;\n  e.x += e.vx;\n  e.y += e.vy;\n}\n"
}

projectiles.length = 0;\n  const pt = Enemy.fromVanilla(262, 100 * 16 - 600, 100 * 16)!;\n  pt.id = 1; enemies.push(pt);\n  run(1);\n  const hooks = enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 263);\n  check('生成 3 钩蔓 263', hooks.length === 3, hooks.length);\n  run(20);\n  check('钩蔓抓到锚点 tile', hooks.every((h) => h.ai0 > 0 && h.ai1 > 0),\n    hooks.map((h) => [h.ai0, h.ai1]));\n  check('钩蔓朝锚点移动', hooks.every((h) => Math.hypot(h.vx, h.vy) > 0.1),\n    hooks.map((h) => Math.hypot(h.vx, h.vy).toFixed(1)));\n  const sx = pt.cx, sy = pt.cy;\n  run(240);\n  check('本体被钩蔓牵引位移', Math.hypot(pt.cx - sx, pt.cy - sy) > 40,\n    [Math.round(pt.cx - sx), Math.round(pt.cy - sy)]);\n  check('钩蔓锚定本体存活', hooks.every((h) => !h.dead && h.hp === h.maxHp));\n  check('钩蔓朝本体旋转', hooks.every((h) => Math.abs(h.spin) > 0.01),\n    hooks.map((h) => h.spin.toFixed(2)));\n  check('一阶段防 36 伤 50', pt.def.defense === 36 && pt.def.damage === 50, [pt.def.defense, pt.def.damage]);\n  const before = projectiles.length;\n  run(400);\n  check('一阶段种子弹幕 275/276/277', projectiles.length > before,\n    projectiles.slice(before).map((p) => p.projId));\n  // 4) 半血以下 → 二阶段\n  pt.hp = pt.maxHp * 0.4;\n  run(2);\n  check('二阶段防 10 伤 70', pt.def.defense === 10 && pt.def.damage === 70, [pt.def.defense, pt.def.damage]);\n  const tents = enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 264 && !e.dead);\n  check('生成 8 触须 264', tents.length === 8, tents.length);\n  let path = 0, lx = pt.cx, ly = pt.cy;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) {\n    run(1);\n    path += Math.hypot(pt.cx - lx, pt.cy - ly);\n    lx = pt.cx; ly = pt.cy;\n  }\n  check('二阶段自主移动(提速 5/7)', path > 100, Math.round(path));\n  run(1200);\n  check('二阶段孢子 265 生成', enemies.some((e) => e.vanillaId === 265 && !e.dead),\n    enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 265).length);\n  // 5) 本体消失 → 钩蔓自毁(原版 StrikeNPCNoInteraction 9999)\n  pt.dead = true;\n  run(2);\n  check('本体消失钩蔓自毁', enemies.filter((e) => e.vanillaId === 263).every((h) => h.dead));\n}\nconsole.log(`\\n结果: ${ok} 通过, ${bad} 失败`);\nprocess.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);\n"
}

Small cleanups in state 3:
- `let num692` can be const; remove `void num692;`.
- Vanilla's double-step in state 3 second branch: I noted it. Actually, I can implement the double-step exactly with a tiny local helper to be truly 1:1. Let me do it — it's a small inline function:

```
const step2 = (v: number, target: number, a: number) => {
  if (v < target) { v += a; if (v < 0 && target > 0) v += a * 2; }
  else if (v > target) { v -= a; if (v > 0 && target < 0) v -= a * 2; }
  return v;
};
```
And use it in state 3's second branch only. Remove the approximation comment.

Also in state 1 vanilla movement (14/0.1) uses single extra step (velocity.X += num687 once more) — that's exactly accelTo ✓. And the !CanHit branch in state 3 (14/0.1) also single-step ✓ (accelTo).
